<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50467087" data-id="50467087" data-tags="Business" data-score="4010" data-comments="3416" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50467087" data-time="1794491549">
<a class="thumb" href="https://apnews.com/article/nvidia-megaspeed-china-singapore-huang-5403f152a601cf82ce939f1d24fffa0f" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=apnews.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>apnews.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apnews.com/article/nvidia-megaspeed-china-singapore-huang-5403f152a601cf82ce939f1d24fffa0f" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang resigns</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋辞职</span></a><span class="dom">apnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession. The departure marks a leadership change at Nvidia.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。这意味着英伟达将发生领导层变动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>4010</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50467087' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3416</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50378228" data-id="50378228" data-tags="Security" data-score="2978" data-comments="747" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228" data-time="1793881142">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sshhole.com/receipts/3bsd-passwd.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sshhole.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sshole</span><span class='zh'>sshole</span></a><span class="dom">sshhole.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016. The issue relates to how Unix historically represented accounts that should never permit login, including accounts marked with an asterisk in the password file. The behavior can therefore expose accounts that were intended to be inaccessible.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。问题与 Unix 处理本应禁止登录的账户的历史方式有关，包括在密码文件中用星号标记的账户。因此，原本应不可访问的账户可能被暴露。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>2978</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>747</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50385272" data-id="50385272" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Code" data-score="1605" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272" data-time="1793917035">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches. The user says this happened without permission and was followed by a large increase in scam emails. They argue that the behavior should be removed rather than controlled by an opt-in or opt-out setting.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。该用户表示，未经允许发生这一行为后，自己收到的诈骗邮件大幅增加。用户认为，这种行为不应通过加入或退出选项控制，而应从系统中彻底移除。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. One World Record prototype is reported to fly more than 300 feet. The Unite model holds a world record for time aloft at 31.2 seconds.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。据称，一款世界纪录原型机可以飞行超过 300 英尺。Unite 机型则以 31.2 秒的滞空时间保持世界纪录。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://status.cloud.microsoft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Service degradation on Microsoft 365</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 服务降级</span></a><span class="dom">status.cloud.microsoft</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft 365 is experiencing service degradation. The available material does not specify which services are affected, the cause, or the expected resolution time. Users may therefore encounter disruptions while the incident is being addressed.</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft 365 正在经历服务降级。现有材料没有说明受影响的具体服务、原因或预计恢复时间。因此，用户在问题处理期间可能会遇到服务中断。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smart home devices should work without the internet</span><span class='zh'>智能家居应当无需互联网也能运行</span></a><span class="dom">docodethatmatters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server. As a result, the home continues operating when the internet goes down. The post presents the other automation rules and two related talks that grew out of this principle.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。因此，即使互联网中断，房屋仍能继续运行。文章还整理了由这一原则延伸出的其他自动化规则和两场相关演讲。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla to wind down Cybertruck production</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉将逐步停止生产 Cybertruck</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027. He said the move will make room for higher production of the newly launched Cybercab, central to Tesla&#x27;s robotaxi plans. Cybertruck sales have remained weak, and it has sold in only a limited number of markets. It remains unclear whether production will move elsewhere or end entirely.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。他称，此举将为提高新款 Cybercab 的产量腾出空间，而 Cybercab 是特斯拉机器人出租车计划的核心车型。Cybertruck 销量长期疲软，目前仅在少数市场销售。该车型是转移到其他工厂生产还是彻底停产，目前尚不清楚。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libghostty 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Libghostty 1.0</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core. Both the library and its C ABI are now stable. The C API has grown from nonexistent to a complete terminal emulator used in production by millions of people across hundreds of free and commercial products.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。该库本身及其 C ABI 现已稳定。其 C API 已从无到有发展为完整的终端模拟器，目前已在数百款免费和商业产品中投入生产，服务数百万人。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1077</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50399933' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>194</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50452455" data-id="50452455" data-tags="AI|Science|Space|Politics" data-score="1027" data-comments="390" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455" data-time="1794398722">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site/forest-vs-lidar.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pietromiotti.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My AI agents found an unmapped Maya site?</span><span class='zh'>我的 AI 代理发现了一个未标记的玛雅遗址？</span></a><span class="dom">pietromiotti.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico. The site had already been mapped by archaeologists and described in Estrada-Belli et al. 2025, but it was not yet listed in Mexico’s official INAH register. The agents therefore rediscovered the site rather than discovering it, and the process took less than 72 hours to reach an official response from the Mexican government.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。考古学家其实已经绘制过该遗址的地图，并在 Estrada-Belli 等人 2025 年的论文中记录了它，只是它尚未列入墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）的官方名录。因此，代理完成的是“重新发现”而非发现；从提出想法到收到墨西哥政府的正式回复耗时不到 72 小时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1027</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>390</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50465829" data-id="50465829" data-tags="Business|Finance|AI|Science" data-score="996" data-comments="904" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465829" data-time="1794484001">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ipo.anthropic.com/assets/share-image-eAr8nJRP.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ipo.anthropic.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic IPO</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 筹备首次公开募股</span></a><span class="dom">ipo.anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information. The company is organized as a public benefit corporation focused on securing AI’s benefits and mitigating its risks. It says AI could accelerate scientific innovation, increase economic growth, help cure major diseases, and address long-standing problems if managed well.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。该公司采用公益公司架构，致力于发挥人工智能的益处并降低其风险。Anthropic 表示，如果得到妥善管理，人工智能可能加速科学创新、提高经济增长率、推动重大疾病治疗，并解决长期未能解决的问题。</span></p>

The available context does not provide further technical details about its implementation or supported services.</span><span class='zh'>AWS Emulator 是一个用于模拟 AWS 服务的项目。现有信息没有提供其实现方式或所支持服务的更多技术细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neil.fraser.name/news/2026/11/10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.name Grandfathered</span><span class='zh'>.name 域名获得保留</span></a><span class="dom">neil.fraser.name</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign. New registrations for third-level .name domains will close, but all existing domains and redirects will remain indefinitely. The reversal followed efforts by domain owners, lawyers, and supporters to challenge the plan through ICANN.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。虽然 .name 三级域名将停止新注册，但现有域名和邮件转发服务将无限期保留。这一转变源于域名所有者、律师和支持者通过 ICANN 发起的共同争取。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>919</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50442736' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>204</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429068" data-id="50429068" data-tags="AI|Finance" data-score="882" data-comments="1008" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429068" data-time="1794247270">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://assets.claude.com/21b3240f9264f8e4c8ab1390e2702cf1401b2367.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks. The company says the models improve coding, reasoning, and vision, and that prerelease testing helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, advance robotics evaluations, and formalize a proof of Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem. Fable 5.2 is positioned as a new high point for coding, finance, and science.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。该公司称，这些模型在编程、推理和视觉方面有所提升，并在预发布测试中帮助工程师完成软件迁移、从零设计三种可工作的电子设备、推进机器人评测，以及形式化费马大定理的证明。Fable 5.2 被定位为编程、金融和科学领域的新标杆。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.rust-lang.org/2026/11/12/Rust-1.100.0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust 1.100</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.rust-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0. Rust is described as a language for building reliable and efficient software. Users with an existing rustup installation can install the new version through rustup.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。Rust 被定位为用于构建可靠、高效软件的编程语言。已经通过 rustup 安装 Rust 的用户可以使用 rustup 获取新版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://8rank.ai/game/apple-intelligence-personal-knowledge-graph" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every Mac comes with an (undocumented) family member classifier</span><span class='zh'>每台 Mac 都自带一个未公开的家庭成员分类器</span></a><span class="dom">8rank.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities. The graph is described as covering contacts, relationships, communications, locations, activities, and timing.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。该图谱据称涵盖联系人、彼此关系、通信、位置、活动及时间信息。文章还分析了这一系统的工作方式，并提供了查看个人设备上该图谱的工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision. During prerelease testing, Anthropic says the models helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, make progress on robotics evaluations, and solve problems in physics. The company also says the models formalized a proof of Fermat’s Last Theorem in 30 hours.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。在预发布测试中，Anthropic 表示这些模型帮助工程师完成软件迁移、设计出三种可运行的电子设备、推进机器人评测，并解决物理学问题。公司还称，模型在 30 小时内形式化完成了费马大定理的证明。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post says that LibreOffice works all 365 days of the year.</span><span class='zh'>这则内容称 LibreOffice 一年 365 天都能正常工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post says that LibreOffice works all 365 days of the year. It presents the statement as a brief comparison or endorsement of the office suite’s reliability.</span><span class='zh'>这则内容称 LibreOffice 一年 365 天都能正常工作。它以简短的对比或宣传方式强调了这套办公软件的可靠性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/nvidia-employees-petition-for-jensen-huangs-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia employees petition to bring back Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达员工请愿要求让黄仁勋回归</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang. The post was submitted before Huang’s resignation by a longtime engineer and asked the board to consider employee views before taking action. Some employees are now asking the board to find a way to bring Huang back despite reports of multiple extramarital affairs and his abrupt resignation.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。该帖子由一名长期任职的工程师在黄仁勋辞职前提交，要求董事会在采取行动前考虑员工意见。尽管有报道称黄仁勋存在多段婚外情并突然辞职，仍有部分员工要求董事会设法让他回归。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>791</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50477231' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>651</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50406670" data-id="50406670" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="781" data-comments="134" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406670" data-time="1794075132">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/index.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.bosscher.ch' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I run on my $10 USD OpenStick homelab</span><span class='zh'>我在 10 美元 OpenStick 家庭实验室上运行的服务</span></a><span class="dom">blog.bosscher.ch</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware. Although the device is less powerful than many modern watches, it can still support enjoyable self-hosting experiments. The project demonstrates how a very inexpensive, tiny computer can serve as an entry point to homelabbing.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。它的性能可能还不如许多现代智能手表，但仍然适合进行有趣的自托管实验。这个项目展示了廉价小设备如何成为入门家庭实验室的起点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>781</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406670' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>134</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50410107" data-id="50410107" data-tags="AI" data-score="732" data-comments="451" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410107" data-time="1794107508">
<a class="thumb" href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://caleb.software/assets/img/claude-to-pi/vsc-disconnected.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='caleb.software' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye Claude, hello Pi</span><span class='zh'>告别 Claude，迎接 Pi</span></a><span class="dom">caleb.software</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription. Despite considering its release an important moment for software development, the author accumulated enough small frustrations to stop using it as the main coding harness. Their development setup is now being migrated to Pi.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。尽管作者认为它的发布可能会成为软件开发史上的重要节点，但长期积累的许多小问题最终让作者不再把它作为主要编程工具。作者目前正将开发环境迁移到 Pi。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase. It supports Rust, Python, JavaScript, React, C, and C++. The project describes its performance as twice that of GPUI.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。它支持 Rust、Python、JavaScript、React、C 和 C++。项目方称其性能是 GPUI 的两倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://postalpro.usps.com/zcb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>USPS now offers official ZIP Code boundary polygons</span><span class='zh'>USPS 现提供官方 ZIP Code 边界多边形</span></a><span class="dom">postalpro.usps.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data. The boundaries can change as delivery locations are added or modified, and the data is updated quarterly with limited distribution to subsidiaries and partners. The dataset is intended for mapping, mailing, marketing, insurance, real estate, logistics, navigation, and transportation applications.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。随着投递地点新增或变更，边界可能发生变化；数据每季度更新一次，并仅向子公司和合作伙伴有限提供。该数据可用于地图、邮寄、营销、保险、房地产、物流、导航和交通等行业。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/simpsoka/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: My $300 embroidery machine needed a subscription, so I built my own app</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的 300 美元刺绣机需要订阅，于是我自己写了应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer built a replacement app for a $300 embroidery machine that required a subscription.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer built a replacement app for a $300 embroidery machine that required a subscription. The project is published as Zigzag. The available description does not provide further technical details about the machine or the application.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。该项目以 Zigzag 的形式发布。现有描述没有提供有关机器或应用的更多技术细节。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>719</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50470488' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>245</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429426" data-id="50429426" data-tags="Privacy|Security|AI|Code" data-score="716" data-comments="147" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426" data-time="1794248678">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Gemini routes personal Drive files and emails to human annotators</span><span class='zh'>Google Gemini 将个人 Drive 文件和邮件交由人工标注员处理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini. This reverses a 2023 promise that personal Gmail, Docs, and Drive content would not be seen by human reviewers. Google now warns users not to connect apps containing confidential information they would not want a reviewer to see.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。这改变了 Google 在 2023 年作出的承诺——当时公司表示个人 Gmail、Docs 和 Drive 内容不会被人工审核员看到。Google 目前警告用户，不要连接包含不希望审核员看到的机密信息的应用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>716</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>147</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50422069" data-id="50422069" data-tags="AI" data-score="708" data-comments="459" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069" data-time="1794207865">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is Opus 5.5 nerfed? Thinking patterns changed on November 3</span><span class='zh'>Opus 5.5 被削弱了吗？11 月 3 日推理模式发生变化</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3. The model name did not change, but the analysis claims that its thinking patterns shifted noticeably. The report does not identify the cause of the change.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。模型名称并未改变，但分析认为其思考模式出现了显著转折。该报告没有说明变化原因。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>708</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>459</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50415628" data-id="50415628" data-tags="AI|Programming|Finance" data-score="707" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628" data-time="1794161767">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.thestateofai.com/media/uploads/33f913ce812c4074d207c01b6df1858a692e6bc3229dadbfcd4f56b61722a538.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thestateofai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">29</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs is pulling Claude Code from its bankers and traders</span><span class='zh'>高盛将从银行家和交易员手中收回 Claude Code</span></a><span class="dom">thestateofai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers. Those employees had used the tools to track client flows and build market models, while the bank had already barred Hong Kong bankers from Claude in April. The guidance followed a review of productivity and risk concerns and came weeks before Goldman was due to act as a lead underwriter for Anthropic&#x27;s IPO.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。此前，这些员工曾用相关工具追踪客户资金流向并构建市场模型；高盛也已于 4 月禁止香港银行家使用 Claude。这项指导是在审查生产力和风险问题后出台的，时间上距离高盛担任 Anthropic IPO 主承销商仅数周。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>707</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>391</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50436640" data-id="50436640" data-tags="Finance|Business|AI" data-score="693" data-comments="242" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50436640" data-time="1794290468">
<a class="thumb" href="https://strandsagents.com/blog/amazon-saves-millions-replacing-sonnet-with-strands-decider/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://strandsagents.com/blog/og/amazon-saves-millions-replacing-sonnet-with-strands-decider.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='strandsagents.com' data-c='#84cc16' data-tag='Finance'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">30</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://strandsagents.com/blog/amazon-saves-millions-replacing-sonnet-with-strands-decider/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Amazon saves millions by replacing Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊用 Strands Decider 替代 Sonnet 4.6，节省数百万美元</span></a><span class="dom">strandsagents.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two Amazon engineers replaced Claude Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider in a major internal ticketing system.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊的两名工程师在一个大型内部工单系统中，用 Strands Decider 替代了 Claude Sonnet 4.6。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two Amazon engineers replaced Claude Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider in a major internal ticketing system. Sonnet had provided a balance of intelligence and cost, but Strands Decider reportedly performed equally well at 98% lower cost. The change is expected to save millions of dollars in annual token expenses.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊的两名工程师在一个大型内部工单系统中，用 Strands Decider 替代了 Claude Sonnet 4.6。Sonnet 原本在智能程度和成本之间取得了平衡，但据称 Strands Decider 在表现相当的情况下将成本降低了 98%。这一调整预计每年可节省数百万美元的 token 费用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>693</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50436640' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>242</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50474959" data-id="50474959" data-tags="Hardware|Privacy" data-score="681" data-comments="200" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474959" data-time="1794520989">
<a class="thumb" href="https://scottjg.com/posts/2026-11-11-reverse-engineering-my-coway-air-purifier-to-ditch-the-cloud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=scottjg.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>scottjg.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottjg.com/posts/2026-11-11-reverse-engineering-my-coway-air-purifier-to-ditch-the-cloud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering my Coway air purifier to ditch the cloud</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程我的 Coway 空气净化器，摆脱云端控制</span></a><span class="dom">scottjg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed. The author describes buying a Coway Airmega to avoid breathing wildfire soot and reverse-engineering it to remove its dependence on cloud services. The project reflects a broader effort to keep household devices locally controlled.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。作者介绍了自己购买 Coway Airmega，并对其进行逆向工程，以摆脱对云端服务的依赖。这一项目体现了将家用设备控制权保留在本地的尝试。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.cbsnews.com/news/united-airlines-bans-ai-agents-booking-flights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://assets3.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/11/11/abfecdbe-5571-42df-95f8-6c22131067aa/thumbnail/1200x630/526b47f241e8e11c31cb47bc82f1f68c/gettyimages-2296986024-1.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='cbsnews.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbsnews.com/news/united-airlines-bans-ai-agents-booking-flights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>United Airlines bans AI agents from booking flights on behalf of travelers</span><span class='zh'>联合航空禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班</span></a><span class="dom">cbsnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>United Airlines said it is banning AI agents from making flight reservations on behalf of travelers.</span><span class='zh'>联合航空表示，将禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>United Airlines said it is banning AI agents from making flight reservations on behalf of travelers. The airline cited security risks and the possibility that such actions could violate federal rules. Consumers are increasingly using agents such as Meta’s Muse and OpenAI’s dots for tasks including shopping and purchases.</span><span class='zh'>联合航空表示，将禁止 AI 代理代旅客预订航班。该航空公司称，这类操作存在安全风险，并可能违反联邦法规。消费者正越来越多地使用 Meta 的 Muse 和 OpenAI 的 dots 等代理来完成商品研究、比价和购买等任务。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>661</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458900' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>688</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50438261" data-id="50438261" data-tags="Education|AI|Code" data-score="655" data-comments="155" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261" data-time="1794305902">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Socratic Tutor</span><span class='zh'>苏格拉底式导师</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture. It is intended for ongoing teaching sessions, not one-off factual questions. When the user’s intent is ambiguous, it first asks whether they want a quick answer or a full tutoring session.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。它适用于持续的教学过程，不适用于一次性的事实问答。当用户意图不明确时，它会先询问用户想要快速答案还是完整的辅导过程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>655</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>155</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50390124" data-id="50390124" data-tags="Privacy|Code" data-score="648" data-comments="424" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124" data-time="1793957232">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS. The report identifies app version 2.40.2 in the Netherlands. One user reported that the app appeared to work on a stock operating system.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。报告涉及荷兰地区的 2.40.2 版本。一名用户表示，该应用在原生操作系统上看起来可以正常运行。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://deepmind.google/blog/introducing-alphaproof-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/hn6AiI6lYFo6chi1mL6uJ9nOW9UXfsAKnSyOApiie2urHNU2tmSPUmGn0hNfWyYLRcsWCGjCIrBZSCt1ihZmvW-LDJPfyolsr5w-yvzlknZVzmaH=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='deepmind.google' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/introducing-alphaproof-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code agents generated more than three million lines of machine-checked Lean proof.</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code 代理生成了超过 300 万行经过机器检查的 Lean 证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code agents generated more than three million lines of machine-checked Lean proof. The proofs verify Signal’s core cryptographic protections down to machine instructions. The result demonstrates the agents’ use in formal verification of security-critical software.</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code 代理生成了超过 300 万行经过机器检查的 Lean 证明。这些证明将 Signal 的核心加密防护验证到了机器指令级别。该成果展示了这类代理在安全关键软件形式化验证中的用途。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.payments.ca/canada-launches-real-time-rail-bringing-247-payment-infrastructure-national-economy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announces launch of Canada&#x27;s Real-Time Rail (RTR) payment system</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路支付系统</span></a><span class="dom">payments.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announced the launch of Canada’s Real-Time Rail, a national payment system for instant clearing and settlement.</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路（RTR），这是一个支持即时清算和结算的全国性支付系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announced the launch of Canada’s Real-Time Rail, a national payment system for instant clearing and settlement. Participating financial institutions, credit unions, and payment service providers can exchange, clear, and settle payments securely in real time, 24 hours a day. The system also provides real-time cash-flow visibility and data-rich messaging through existing banking and payment channels.</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路（RTR），这是一个支持即时清算和结算的全国性支付系统。参与其中的金融机构、信用合作社和支付服务商可以全年全天候安全地实时交换、清算和结算付款。该系统还通过现有银行和支付渠道提供实时现金流可见性以及包含丰富数据的消息传递。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://michael.bacarella.com/2026/11/10/false-smoke-alarm-jane-street-datacenter/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A false smoke alarm almost drowned Jane Street&#x27;s only NYC datacenter</span><span class='zh'>一次误报的烟雾警报差点淹没 Jane Street 在纽约唯一的数据中心</span></a><span class="dom">michael.bacarella.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Jane Street systems-team account describes a false smoke alarm at the firm’s only New York datacenter.</span><span class='zh'>Jane Street 系统团队的一篇回忆讲述了该公司纽约唯一数据中心发生误报烟雾警报的经过。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Jane Street systems-team account describes a false smoke alarm at the firm’s only New York datacenter. The facility lost cooling, and staff rushed to unplug every power cord before the sprinkler system activated. The incident is presented as an example of the hands-on work required when a serious infrastructure failure nearly occurred.</span><span class='zh'>Jane Street 系统团队的一篇回忆讲述了该公司纽约唯一数据中心发生误报烟雾警报的经过。机房一度失去冷却，员工赶在喷淋系统启动前冲进去拔掉所有电源线。这个事件展示了基础设施险些发生严重故障时，系统团队必须亲自处理现场问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>575</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50455394' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>272</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50397429" data-id="50397429" data-tags="AI|Data" data-score="574" data-comments="146" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50397429" data-time="1793994531">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pwmfmi47/production/9a5d7191d614c824c822ec90672adaf424699d3d-2400x1256.png?w=1200' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='hightouch.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">39</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Jev that can ask Opus for help gives Opus accuracy at 4x lower cost</span><span class='zh'>能向 Opus 求助的 Jev，让 Opus 以低四倍成本达到同等准确率</span></a><span class="dom">hightouch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%. A hybrid that combines Jev with Claude Opus matched frontier-level accuracy at more than four times lower cost. Identity resolution maps data records to unique real-world people.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。将 Jev 与 Claude Opus 结合的混合方案达到了前沿模型水平的准确率，而成本降低了四倍以上。身份解析的任务是将数据记录映射到现实世界中唯一的个人。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mcmillen.dev/sigbovik/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>93% of paint splatters are valid Perl programs (2019)</span><span class='zh'>93% 的颜料飞溅都是有效的 Perl 程序（2019）</span></a><span class="dom">mcmillen.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A paper investigates whether it is possible to smear paint on a wall without producing a valid Perl program.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文研究了这样的问题：把颜料涂抹到墙上时，是否可能不生成有效的 Perl 程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A paper investigates whether it is possible to smear paint on a wall without producing a valid Perl program. Its stated result is that 93% of paint splatters are valid Perl programs, accompanied by a gallery of the resulting programs. The work presents the question as a long-standing open problem in programming languages.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文研究了这样的问题：把颜料涂抹到墙上时，是否可能不生成有效的 Perl 程序。论文给出的结果是，93% 的颜料飞溅都属于有效 Perl 程序，并配有这些程序的展示画廊。作者把这个问题称为编程语言领域长期存在的开放问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>566</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50459745' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>79</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50471431" data-id="50471431" data-tags="AI" data-score="559" data-comments="349" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471431" data-time="1794506508">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-hydrogen/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-4-hydrogen__key-art__lockup__16-9.width-1300.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-hydrogen/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Hydrogen as a fast, cost-efficient model for agentic workflows and everyday tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Hydrogen as a fast, cost-efficient model for agentic workflows and everyday tasks. The company positions it as a daily-use model for reliable agentic work. Google also says Hydrogen brings advanced intelligence to users broadly.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，将其定位为面向智能体工作流和日常任务的快速、高性价比模型。公司将其定位为可日常使用、适合可靠智能体工作的模型。Google 还表示，Hydrogen 将先进的智能能力带给更广泛的用户。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>559</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471431' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>349</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50456791" data-id="50456791" data-tags="AI" data-score="554" data-comments="157" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50456791" data-time="1794421962">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/gemini-decisions-api/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini__decisions__blog-header_2784x1566.width-1300.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">42</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/gemini-decisions-api/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini Decisions API</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Decisions API</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google introduced the Gemini Decisions API, powered by its Gemini Decision 1 model.</span><span class='zh'>Google 发布了由 Gemini Decision 1 模型驱动的 Gemini Decisions API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google introduced the Gemini Decisions API, powered by its Gemini Decision 1 model. It can evaluate up to 1,000 yes/no, multiple-choice, or rubric-scored questions in a single pass across text, images, audio, video, and PDFs. Google describes the decisions as fast and calibrated for its applications.</span><span class='zh'>Google 发布了由 Gemini Decision 1 模型驱动的 Gemini Decisions API。它可以在一次处理中，针对文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF，评估最多 1,000 个是非题、选择题或按评分标准判定的问题。Google 将其描述为速度快且经过校准的决策 API。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theautopian.com/scion-is-back-and-its-going-to-sell-a-freaking-kei-truck-in-america/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scion is back and is going to sell a kei truck in America</span><span class='zh'>Scion 回归，将在美国销售一款 Kei 微型卡车</span></a><span class="dom">theautopian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles. The new “Scion by Toyota” is being positioned as a “freedom lab” focused on challenging conventional automotive thinking, and its initial offerings include a kei truck, a bike, and a hybrid side-by-side. The brand’s new direction is a sharp departure from its former role as a quirky, budget-oriented line aimed largely at younger buyers.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。全新的“Scion by Toyota”被定位为一个挑战传统汽车思维的“自由实验室”，首批产品包括一款 Kei 微型卡车、一辆自行车和一款混合动力并排式越野车。品牌的新方向与过去面向年轻消费者、主打古怪且价格亲民车型的定位大相径庭。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>553</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50445401' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>397</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50446520" data-id="50446520" data-tags="AI" data-score="550" data-comments="551" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50446520" data-time="1794345903">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/11/10/apple-has-new-mac-app-coming-to-make-running-local-ai-models-easy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple has new Mac app coming to make running local AI models easy</span><span class='zh'>Apple 将推出让本地运行 AI 模型更简单的 Mac 应用</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs. Evidence of the app was found by leaker Aaron Perris. The project comes as Macs are increasingly being used to run models locally, including on the Mac Studio and Mac mini.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。泄密者 Aaron Perris 发现了这款应用的相关证据。随着 Mac Studio、Mac mini 等设备被越来越多地用于本地运行模型，该项目正处于这一趋势之中。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>550</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50446520' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>551</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50406111" data-id="50406111" data-tags="Privacy" data-score="534" data-comments="359" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406111" data-time="1794071196">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">45</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/nov/06/struggling-to-spell-male-chauvinist-pig-apple-grey-list-means-your-iphone-wont-help-you" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple’s grey list: Words and phrases your iPhone won’t help you spell</span><span class='zh'>苹果的“灰名单”：iPhone 不会帮你拼出的词语和短语</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Research found that Apple restricts predictive-text completion for more than 100,000 words and phrases.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，苹果限制了超过 10 万个词语和短语的预测文本补全功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Research found that Apple restricts predictive-text completion for more than 100,000 words and phrases. The restrictions affect swipe typing, in which users glide a finger across the keyboard instead of tapping each character. As a result, an iPhone may refuse to complete some unusual or sensitive phrases even when users try to enter them by gesture.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，苹果限制了超过 10 万个词语和短语的预测文本补全功能。这些限制会影响滑行输入，即用户用手指划过键盘，而不是逐字点击。结果是，即使用户尝试通过滑行输入，iPhone 也可能不会补全某些不寻常或敏感的短语。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wsj.com/business/nvidia-is-preparing-to-oust-nvidia-ceo-jensen-huang-30f421ad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia is preparing to oust Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达正准备让 Jensen Huang 离任</span></a><span class="dom">wsj.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia is reportedly preparing to remove Jensen Huang from his position.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，英伟达正准备让 Jensen Huang 离任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia is reportedly preparing to remove Jensen Huang from his position. No further reliable details are available.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，英伟达正准备让 Jensen Huang 离任。现有信息没有提供更多可靠细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://starlink.com/mini-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2</span></a><span class="dom">starlink.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2 is presented as providing fast, reliable internet both at home and on the go.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2 is presented as providing fast, reliable internet both at home and on the go. The service is intended to support streaming, video calls, gaming, and other high-speed connectivity needs, including in rural and remote locations.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。该服务支持流媒体、视频通话、游戏等高速联网需求，也面向乡村和偏远地区。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://antigravity.google/blog/gemini-4-hydrogen-in-google-antigravity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen and Orchestrator</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Hydrogen 与 Orchestrator</span></a><span class="dom">antigravity.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Antigravity is rolling out Gemini 4 Hydrogen together with a new model-selector option called Orchestrator.</span><span class='zh'>Google Antigravity 正在推出 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，以及模型选择器中的新选项 Orchestrator。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Antigravity is rolling out Gemini 4 Hydrogen together with a new model-selector option called Orchestrator. Orchestrator pairs Hydrogen with Google’s strongest models for long-horizon tasks, including Gemini 4 Carbon. The combination is intended to balance quality, efficiency, and latency.</span><span class='zh'>Google Antigravity 正在推出 Gemini 4 Hydrogen，以及模型选择器中的新选项 Orchestrator。Orchestrator 会将 Hydrogen 与包括 Gemini 4 Carbon 在内的 Google 最强模型搭配，用于长时程任务。该组合旨在平衡质量、效率和延迟。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://e-means.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://e-means.github.io/assets/emeans_vs_lloyd_readme_v9.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='e-means.github.io' data-c='#6366f1' data-tag='Data'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://e-means.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>E-means: Outperforming k-means in one-tenth the epochs</span><span class='zh'>E-means：用十分之一的训练轮次超越 k-means</span></a><span class="dom">e-means.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>E-means is presented as an alternative to Lloyd&#x27;s k-means algorithm.</span><span class='zh'>E-means 被介绍为 Lloyd k-means 算法的一种替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>E-means is presented as an alternative to Lloyd&#x27;s k-means algorithm. Across a broad range of scales and datasets, it reached lower test loss than Lloyd&#x27;s method in 1.2 to 3 epochs, compared with 25 epochs. The reported end-to-end speedup is 10–45× over Flash-KMeans on GPUs and 8–15× over Faiss on CPUs.</span><span class='zh'>E-means 被介绍为 Lloyd k-means 算法的一种替代方案。在多种规模和数据集上，E-means 用 1.2 至 3 个训练轮次就达到了低于 Lloyd 方法的测试损失，而后者需要 25 个轮次。报告称，端到端速度相比 GPU 上的 Flash-KMeans 提升 10–45 倍，相比 CPU 上的 Faiss 提升 8–15 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>494</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429734' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>78</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50481187" data-id="50481187" data-tags="AI" data-score="484" data-comments="383" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50481187" data-time="1794565093">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102549/acbc92e00cee3a3f/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Synaptics patent claims stop work on DisplayLink drivers</span><span class='zh'>Synaptics 的专利主张叫停 DisplayLink 驱动开发</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate. Synaptics, which acquired DisplayLink in 2022, responded with patent-infringement claims, a demand to stop the unauthorized implementation, and warnings of costly legal penalties. Linux users of the company’s docks therefore appear likely to remain without feature parity for now.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。DisplayLink 于 2022 年被 Synaptics 收购，而 Synaptics 随后以专利侵权为由要求停止未经授权的实现，并警告可能面临高昂法律处罚。因此，相关扩展坞的 Linux 用户目前似乎仍将无法获得与其他平台相当的功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>481</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443824' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>267</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50468888" data-id="50468888" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="466" data-comments="180" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888" data-time="1794496990">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">54</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers. It uses custom kernels, memory optimization, and weight streaming, with claims measured on the actual hardware. On a Xiaomi TV Box S 3rd Gen with about 1 GB of usable RAM, the project reports running models ranging from 260K to 16B parameters.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。项目使用定制内核、内存优化和权重流式传输，并在真实硬件上测量各项结果。在可用内存约 1 GB 的 Xiaomi TV Box S 第三代上，项目报告称可运行参数量从 26 万到 160 亿不等的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>466</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>180</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50368481" data-id="50368481" data-tags="Programming" data-score="465" data-comments="62" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50368481" data-time="1793811907">
<a class="thumb" href="https://declarative-dragging.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://declarative-dragging.github.io/link-preview.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='declarative-dragging.github.io' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">55</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://declarative-dragging.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Declarative Dragging</span><span class='zh'>声明式拖拽</span></a><span class="dom">declarative-dragging.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Declarative dragging proposes that interface authors define which future model states an element may reach by dragging.</span><span class='zh'>“声明式拖拽”提出，界面作者可以通过指定元素拖动后允许到达的模型状态来定义拖拽行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Declarative dragging proposes that interface authors define which future model states an element may reach by dragging. This makes a diagram element draggable by describing its allowed outcomes rather than implementing dragging behavior directly. The work was named the Best Paper at UIST 2026.</span><span class='zh'>“声明式拖拽”提出，界面作者可以通过指定元素拖动后允许到达的模型状态来定义拖拽行为。这样，创建可拖动的图表元素不必直接实现拖拽逻辑，而是描述其允许的结果。该研究获得了 UIST 2026 最佳论文奖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>465</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50368481' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>62</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50478609" data-id="50478609" data-tags="DevOps" data-score="462" data-comments="306" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478609" data-time="1794546503">
<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/MSFT365Status/status/2121079061774491735" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#14b8a6' data-tag='DevOps'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=twitter.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>twitter.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">56</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/MSFT365Status/status/2121079061774491735" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft Outlook/365 still down after 12 hours</span><span class='zh'>Microsoft Outlook/365 持续中断 12 小时后仍未恢复</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft said changes intended to improve serviceability were not producing the expected results during the Outlook/365 outage. The company was identifying alternative workstreams to increase capacity amid sustained traffic-management imbalances. The service had remained disrupted for more than 12 hours.</span><span class='zh'>微软表示，在 Outlook/365 宕机期间，为改善服务能力而实施的变更没有达到预期效果。面对持续的流量管理失衡，公司正在寻找其他工作方案以提升容量。服务中断当时已持续超过 12 个小时。</span></p>
