<a class="thumb" href="https://www.watched.earth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=watched.earth&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>watched.earth</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.watched.earth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Every satellite that passed over any place this week, and its photos</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：查看本周经过任意地点的每颗卫星及其照片</span></a><span class="dom">watched.earth</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Watched lets users enter a location and find every satellite that passed over it in the previous seven days, then retrieve Sentinel-2, Landsat, and NASA imagery from those passes.</span><span class='zh'>Watched 允许用户输入地点，查找过去七天内经过该地点的所有卫星，并获取这些过境对应的 Sentinel-2、Landsat 和 NASA 影像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Watched lets users enter a location and find every satellite that passed over it in the previous seven days, then retrieve Sentinel-2, Landsat, and NASA imagery from those passes. Its browser-based SGP4 search processes the CelesTrak catalog in about half a second, while roughly 17,000 orbital objects are rendered on the GPU. The project reports no missed passes in tests against brute-force searches and says it does not store locations.</span><span class='zh'>Watched 允许用户输入地点，查找过去七天内经过该地点的所有卫星，并获取这些过境对应的 Sentinel-2、Landsat 和 NASA 影像。它在浏览器中使用 SGP4 处理 CelesTrak 目录，耗时约半秒，同时在 GPU 上绘制约 1.7 万个轨道目标。项目称，与暴力搜索对比测试未漏掉任何过境，并表示不会存储用户位置。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/statement-from-nvidia-board-of-directors" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Board of Directors Is Investigating Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会正在调查黄仁勋</span></a><span class="dom">nvidianews.nvidia.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board has initiated an independent investigation after reports that founder and CEO Jensen Huang was targeted by a foreign intelligence effort.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会在相关报道指出创始人兼 CEO 黄仁勋成为外国情报行动目标后，启动了独立调查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board has initiated an independent investigation after reports that founder and CEO Jensen Huang was targeted by a foreign intelligence effort. A committee of independent directors will oversee the investigation with assistance from outside independent counsel. The available statement does not provide further findings.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会在相关报道指出创始人兼 CEO 黄仁勋成为外国情报行动目标后，启动了独立调查。调查将由独立董事组成的委员会负责，并由外部独立法律顾问协助。现有声明没有提供进一步调查结论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/11/06/every-windows-11-app-should-kill-electron-and-discord-just-showed-why-no-starting-screen-and-70-less-ram-even-with-two-accounts-signed-in/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every Windows 11 app should kill Electron, and Discord just showed why</span><span class='zh'>每款 Windows 11 应用都该淘汰 Electron，而 Discord 展示了原因</span></a><span class="dom">windowslatest.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discord&#x27;s new Rust client loads the friends list before the existing Electron app gets past its loading screen and skips the Starting screen.</span><span class='zh'>Discord 的新 Rust 客户端在现有 Electron 客户端还没通过加载界面时就已载入好友列表，并跳过了 Starting 界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discord&#x27;s new Rust client loads the friends list before the existing Electron app gets past its loading screen and skips the Starting screen. In a cold-start test, the Rust-based client used 290.2 MB of RAM with two accounts signed in. Discord employees confirmed that both applications were launched from a cold start, while a video showing the comparison received 2.3 million views at the time reported.</span><span class='zh'>Discord 的新 Rust 客户端在现有 Electron 客户端还没通过加载界面时就已载入好友列表，并跳过了 Starting 界面。在冷启动测试中，Rust 客户端同时登录两个账号时占用 290.2 MB 内存。Discord 员工确认两款应用都是冷启动，该对比视频当时已获得 230 万次观看。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>457</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50403507' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>490</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50482379" data-id="50482379" data-tags="Science" data-score="454" data-comments="313" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50482379" data-time="1794571824">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.blender.org/download/releases/5-3/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender 5.3 released with Gaussian Splat support</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.3 发布，支持 Gaussian Splat</span></a><span class="dom">blender.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blender 5.3 adds native support for 3D Gaussian Splats.</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.3 新增了对 3D Gaussian Splat 的原生支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blender 5.3 adds native support for 3D Gaussian Splats. Users can import splats as point clouds, experiment with Geometry Nodes, preview them in Workbench, and render them with EEVEE or Cycles. The release also includes DLSS denoising, light dispersion, an EEVEE fisheye effect, Outliner sorting, and ripple video editing.</span><span class='zh'>Blender 5.3 新增了对 3D Gaussian Splat 的原生支持。用户可以将其作为点云导入，使用 Geometry Nodes 进行实验，在 Workbench 中预览，并通过 EEVEE 或 Cycles 渲染。该版本还加入 DLSS 降噪、光色散、EEVEE 鱼眼效果、大纲视图排序和波纹视频编辑等功能。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sources say former Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang had a relationship with a second employee.</span><span class='zh'>消息人士称，英伟达前 CEO 黄仁勋曾与第二名员工有关系。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.vulncheck.com/blog/anthropic-glasswing-november-update" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.vulncheck.com/_og/s/o_6eba28d38c5a4083.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='vulncheck.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">63</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vulncheck.com/blog/anthropic-glasswing-november-update" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tracking Anthropic-discovered vulnerabilities</span><span class='zh'>追踪 Anthropic 发现的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">vulncheck.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Seven months into Project Glasswing, VulnCheck says it has tracked 442 CVEs attributed to Anthropic, representing about 0.5% of all CVEs published that year.</span><span class='zh'>在 Project Glasswing 启动七个月后，VulnCheck 表示已追踪到 442 个归因于 Anthropic 的 CVE，约占当年发布的全部 CVE 的 0.5%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Seven months into Project Glasswing, VulnCheck says it has tracked 442 CVEs attributed to Anthropic, representing about 0.5% of all CVEs published that year. Only two were identified as exploited in the wild, while 7,349 of Anthropic&#x27;s 37,966 reported findings had been made public and 217 were marked fixed. The project began with Anthropic&#x27;s claim that it had found thousands of additional high- and critical-severity vulnerabilities in open-source software.</span><span class='zh'>在 Project Glasswing 启动七个月后，VulnCheck 表示已追踪到 442 个归因于 Anthropic 的 CVE，约占当年发布的全部 CVE 的 0.5%。其中只有两个被确认已在现实环境中遭到利用；Anthropic 报告的 37,966 项发现中，有 7,349 项已公开，217 项被标记为已修复。该项目源于 Anthropic 的说法：其发现了数千个开源软件中的新增高危和严重漏洞。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>447</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50447074' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>219</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50453288" data-id="50453288" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="434" data-comments="82" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50453288" data-time="1794404369">
<a class="thumb" href="https://av.github.io/safe-sand/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=av.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>av.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">64</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://av.github.io/safe-sand/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hyper-realistic sand with WebGL2</span><span class='zh'>使用 WebGL2 实现超逼真的沙子模拟</span></a><span class="dom">av.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser-based sand simulator uses WebGL2 and JavaScript to model moving grains.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器沙子模拟器使用 WebGL2 和 JavaScript 模拟沙粒运动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser-based sand simulator uses WebGL2 and JavaScript to model moving grains. Users can draw and push the sand, tilt a phone to slide the grains, or shake the device to scatter them. It supports touch and motion controls on phones and tablets, as well as desktop browsers.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器沙子模拟器使用 WebGL2 和 JavaScript 模拟沙粒运动。用户可以绘制并推动沙子，在手机上倾斜设备让沙粒滑动，或摇晃设备将其散开。它支持手机和平板上的触控与动作控制，也可在桌面浏览器中运行。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Get your coding agent off ~2019 JavaScript, onto modern web primitives</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让编码代理摆脱约 2019 年的 JavaScript，转向现代 Web 原语</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs. Examples include CSS highlights, dialog and showModal(), and field-sizing, which can replace common JavaScript-heavy patterns. The open-source release contains more than 200 expert-written guides, developed with Firefox, MDN, and Edge, and is designed for agent skills and retrieval-augmented tooling.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。例如，CSS Highlights、dialog 与 showModal()、field-sizing 都可以替代常见的重 JavaScript 实现。该开源项目目前包含 200 多份由专家编写的指南，并与 Firefox、MDN 和 Edge 合作开发，面向代理技能和检索增强工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>423</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50438318" data-id="50438318" data-tags="AI|Programming|Web" data-score="421" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438318" data-time="1794306358">
<a class="thumb" href="https://evanxmerz.com/post/ai-usage-generates-a-fragile-codebase/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.evanxmerz.com/images/ai-codebases-are-a-house-of-cards.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='evanxmerz.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">66</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://evanxmerz.com/post/ai-usage-generates-a-fragile-codebase/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI usage generates a fragile codebase</span><span class='zh'>AI 使用会产生脆弱的代码库</span></a><span class="dom">evanxmerz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author says that at Meta, they generated at least 90% of their code with AI during their final months, yet did not see output multiply.</span><span class='zh'>作者表示，在 Meta 任职的最后几个月里，自己至少有 90% 的代码由 AI 生成，但产出并没有因此成倍增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author says that at Meta, they generated at least 90% of their code with AI during their final months, yet did not see output multiply. They observed AI-assisted code that was brittle, created subtle technical debt, and sometimes contained obvious issues missed during review. One example used JavaScript mouseover events instead of the scalable CSS :hover selector.</span><span class='zh'>作者表示，在 Meta 任职的最后几个月里，自己至少有 90% 的代码由 AI 生成，但产出并没有因此成倍增加。作者观察到一些高度依赖 AI 的代码较为脆弱，会造成隐性的技术债务，有时连明显问题也未被优秀程序员发现。其中一个例子用 JavaScript 鼠标悬停事件实现 CSS 效果，而不是使用更具扩展性的 :hover 选择器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>421</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438318' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>431</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50443427" data-id="50443427" data-tags="Startups" data-score="418" data-comments="100" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443427" data-time="1794331874">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.val.town/winding-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Val Town is winding down</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 即将停止运营</span></a><span class="dom">blog.val.town</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service. The team thanked users for trusting the platform and apologized that it could no longer continue serving them. The announcement does not provide further details about the shutdown timeline or transition.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。团队感谢用户对平台的信任，并为无法继续提供服务表示歉意。公告没有进一步说明停运时间表或迁移安排。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://asahilinux.org/2026/11/progress-report-7-3/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.3</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 7.3 进展</span></a><span class="dom">asahilinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux 7.3 has been released, and Asahi Linux continues upstreaming support for Apple Silicon.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 7.3 已发布，Asahi Linux 仍在持续将 Apple Silicon 支持代码合并到上游。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux 7.3 has been released, and Asahi Linux continues upstreaming support for Apple Silicon. Two major drivers, macaudio and avd, joined the upstream work this cycle. Getting macaudio ready required substantial ALSA changes and modifications to Apple’s speaker and headset amplifier support, with the driver and Devicetree patches totaling about 3,100 lines.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 7.3 已发布，Asahi Linux 仍在持续将 Apple Silicon 支持代码合并到上游。本周期有两个重要驱动加入上游工作：macaudio 和 avd。为了准备 macaudio，项目对 ALSA 进行了大量修改，也改造了 Apple 扬声器和耳机放大器的支持；相关驱动与设备树补丁共约 3100 行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>417</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50401867' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>396</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50411961" data-id="50411961" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="404" data-comments="181" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961" data-time="1794133324">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/synchopate/llama-modchip" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip: 284B DeepSeek on 64GB RAM, no GPU</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip：仅用 64GB 内存、无需 GPU 运行 284B DeepSeek</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM. Its project page claims that a 284B DeepSeek-V4-Flash model can run on a 64GB CPU-only system at a 256K context length, with reported prompt-processing speeds of 44.4 tok/s and generation speeds of 9.1–14.7 tok/s. It supports x86_64 and ARM64 processors and also targets 30B MoE models.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。项目页面称，284B 参数的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 可在仅配备 64GB 内存、没有 GPU 的 CPU 系统上运行，支持 256K 上下文，提示处理速度为 44.4 token/s，生成速度为 9.1–14.7 token/s。该项目支持 x86_64 和 ARM64 处理器，也面向 30B MoE 模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>404</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>181</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50388001" data-id="50388001" data-tags="Science" data-score="403" data-comments="327" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50388001" data-time="1793937628">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/us/us-faa-says-it-will-phase-out-floppy-disks-air-traffic-control-by-2028-2026-11-05/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US FAA says it will phase out floppy disks from air traffic control by 2028</span><span class='zh'>美国 FAA 将在 2028 年前逐步淘汰空管系统中的软盘</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The US Federal Aviation Administration plans to phase out floppy disks from its air-traffic-control systems by 2028.</span><span class='zh'>美国联邦航空管理局计划在 2028 年前逐步淘汰空中交通管制系统中的软盘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The US Federal Aviation Administration plans to phase out floppy disks from its air-traffic-control systems by 2028. The move will replace a legacy storage technology still used in parts of air-traffic operations.</span><span class='zh'>美国联邦航空管理局计划在 2028 年前逐步淘汰空中交通管制系统中的软盘。这一举措将替换部分航空交通运营中仍在使用的老旧存储技术。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://maxxbench.unlap.ai/research/why-maxxbench/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://maxxbench.unlap.ai/social-card.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='maxxbench.unlap.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">71</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://maxxbench.unlap.ai/research/why-maxxbench/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MaxxBench – &quot;Nice DeepSWE score. But is it true?&quot;</span><span class='zh'>MaxxBench：“DeepSWE 得分不错，但它是真的吗？”</span></a><span class="dom">maxxbench.unlap.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MaxxBench changed the requirements of familiar engineering tasks in a coding benchmark to test whether models would follow the new instructions.</span><span class='zh'>MaxxBench 修改了编码基准中熟悉工程任务的要求，用来测试模型是否会遵循新指令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MaxxBench changed the requirements of familiar engineering tasks in a coding benchmark to test whether models would follow the new instructions. The researchers found cases where a model understood and correctly implemented the changed requirement, then removed its own correct code after another model supplied the benchmark’s original answer. The results expose a gap between recognizing a requirement and consistently acting on it.</span><span class='zh'>MaxxBench 修改了编码基准中熟悉工程任务的要求，用来测试模型是否会遵循新指令。研究人员发现，有时模型明白并正确实现了新要求，却在另一个模型给出基准原始答案后删除了自己的正确代码。这些结果显示，模型理解要求与始终按照要求执行之间存在差距。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>392</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406222' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>105</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50472490" data-id="50472490" data-tags="Space|Science" data-score="387" data-comments="185" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472490" data-time="1794510333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/4eu4Cs6lY_bS29DXaQHZWCxQBrs=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1024x770:1025x771)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/ab/21/ab21fe86-4d88-46f9-bcc2-d385adaa2ceb/noirlab2626c.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='smithsonianmag.com' data-c='#818cf8' data-tag='Space'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">72</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dwarf planet candidate appears which takes roughly 280 years to orbit our sun</span><span class='zh'>一颗矮行星候选体出现了，绕太阳一周约需 280 年</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. It appears unusually large and would take roughly 280 years to orbit the Sun. Its size still needs to be determined before astronomers can classify it as a dwarf planet.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。它看起来异常大，绕太阳一周大约需要 280 年。在将其归类为矮行星之前，天文学家仍需确定它的大小。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://gatezh.com/brand/share.381cc582d58a3410686473b92c1d944fbc83dc59a7e926952aeb6c5c0d8a6ef0.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='gatezh.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">73</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protecting SSH Keys with macOS Secure Enclave</span><span class='zh'>使用 macOS 安全隔区保护 SSH 密钥</span></a><span class="dom">gatezh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications. It is intended to prevent malicious software that can read the filesystem from simply copying private keys from ~/.ssh/. The approach is motivated in part by the risk of running untrusted code from fake interview assignments.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。它旨在防止能够读取文件系统的恶意软件直接复制 ~/.ssh/ 中的私钥。作者提到，虚假面试作业要求用户克隆并运行代码的案例，也是采用这一方案的原因之一。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ketchup3d.github.io/app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KetchUp – Open-source SketchUp alternative built with Rust and WebGPU</span><span class='zh'>KetchUp：用 Rust 和 WebGPU 构建的开源 SketchUp 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">ketchup3d.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KetchUp is a free, open-source 3D modeling tool aimed at woodworking and furniture design.</span><span class='zh'>KetchUp 是一款面向木工和家具设计的免费开源 3D 建模工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KetchUp is a free, open-source 3D modeling tool aimed at woodworking and furniture design. It supports precise dimensions and runs on Windows, macOS, Linux, and in a browser. Users can draw basic shapes and freehand outlines, then use operations such as Push/Pull, move, scale, offset, and painting to create forms.</span><span class='zh'>KetchUp 是一款面向木工和家具设计的免费开源 3D 建模工具。它支持精确尺寸，可运行于 Windows、macOS、Linux 以及浏览器中。用户可以绘制基本图形和自由手绘轮廓，再通过推拉、移动、缩放、偏移和绘色等操作创建模型。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=pron.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>pron.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">78</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing correct software is hard and math alone won’t help us (2016)</span><span class='zh'>写出正确的软件很难，光靠数学也无济于事（2016）</span></a><span class="dom">pron.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult. It reviews results from computability and complexity theory, arguing that complete understanding of a program—needed to establish correctness—is fundamentally at odds with the nature of programs. The piece expands a Curry On talk on computational complexity and software verification, with additional proofs and results.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。文章回顾可计算性理论和复杂性理论的成果，认为要证明正确性所需的完整理解，与程序本身的性质存在根本冲突。文章扩展了作者在 Curry On 大会关于计算复杂性和软件验证的演讲，并加入了更多证明和结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>358</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465352' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>169</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50478515" data-id="50478515" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="356" data-comments="217" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515" data-time="1794545693">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ice-wm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ice-wm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">79</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceWM</span><span class='zh'>IceWM</span></a><span class="dom">ice-wm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System. It provides a taskbar, keyboard and mouse controls, dynamic menus, multiple-monitor support through RandR and Xinerama, themes, and optional session and wallpaper managers. Created by Marko Maček in 1997 using C++, it is now maintained on GitHub and is available on many Linux distributions and BSD systems.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。它提供任务栏、键盘和鼠标控制、动态菜单，以及通过 RandR 和 Xinerama 实现的多显示器支持，还支持主题和可选的会话、壁纸管理器。IceWM 由 Marko Maček 于 1997 年使用 C++ 创建，目前在 GitHub 上维护，并可用于多种 Linux 发行版和 BSD 系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>356</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>217</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50462177" data-id="50462177" data-tags="Security" data-score="355" data-comments="191" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50462177" data-time="1794449471">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1794425853-threat-update-kics-alert_custom.jpeg?fm=webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wiz.io' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">80</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Critical RCE Vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server</span><span class='zh'>Apache HTTP Server 存在严重远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2. The flaw affects the assign_headers() function when it handles shared resources, and a remote unauthenticated attacker could use it to corrupt heap memory and execute arbitrary code. Wiz reports that 64% of cloud environments have vulnerable resources; no public exploit or in-the-wild exploitation had been reported at publication time.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。该漏洞出现在 assign_headers() 函数处理共享资源时，未经身份验证的远程攻击者可能借此破坏堆内存并执行任意代码。Wiz 报告称，64% 的云环境存在易受攻击的资源；截至发布时，尚无公开漏洞利用程序或野外攻击报告。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>355</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50462177' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>191</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50416158" data-id="50416158" data-tags="Security" data-score="348" data-comments="258" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50416158" data-time="1794165632">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mailroute.net/blog/outlook-rejecting-email-without-dmarc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mailroute.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mailroute.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mailroute.net/blog/outlook-rejecting-email-without-dmarc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Outlook.com is rejecting email from domains without DMARC</span><span class='zh'>Outlook.com 拒收未配置 DMARC 的域名邮件</span></a><span class="dom">mailroute.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Since November 5, 2026, Outlook.com and Hotmail have been bouncing some messages sent from domains without a DMARC record.</span><span class='zh'>自 2026 年 11 月 5 日起，Outlook.com 和 Hotmail 开始退回部分来自未配置 DMARC 记录域名的邮件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Since November 5, 2026, Outlook.com and Hotmail have been bouncing some messages sent from domains without a DMARC record. The change affects small senders as well as companies sending mail to outlook.com, hotmail.com, live.com, or msn.com addresses. The reported fix is to publish a single DMARC record in DNS.</span><span class='zh'>自 2026 年 11 月 5 日起，Outlook.com 和 Hotmail 开始退回部分来自未配置 DMARC 记录域名的邮件。这一变化不仅影响大型企业，也影响向 outlook.com、hotmail.com、live.com 或 msn.com 地址发信的小型发件方。据报道，解决办法是在 DNS 中发布一条 DMARC 记录。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>348</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50416158' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>258</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50475713" data-id="50475713" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="338" data-comments="104" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713" data-time="1794524777">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.hashnode.com/uploads/covers/6aea03219fa4aa99923b5acf/de5ebdea-3436-4b9a-b3f2-e9910f3a0abc.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edge0.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">82</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Edge0-145B: Frontier AI Capabilities on Your Own Computer</span><span class='zh'>Edge0-145B：在自己的电脑上运行前沿 AI 能力</span></a><span class="dom">edge0.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools. Edge0-145B is described as an open-source, on-device model with 145 billion parameters for coding, reasoning, and agent tasks. Local inference avoids cloud-model token fees, but large models still require substantial memory, bandwidth, and power.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。Edge0-145B 被称为首个开源的 1450 亿参数设备端模型，可用于编程、推理和智能体任务。本地推理无需支付云模型的 token 费用，但大型模型仍需要大量内存、带宽和电力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50475713' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50453474" data-id="50453474" data-tags="Privacy" data-score="338" data-comments="237" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50453474" data-time="1794405365">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">83</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/tech/1012372/tv-privacy-acr-questions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I asked every TV manufacturer 17 questions about how they spy on you</span><span class='zh'>我向每家电视制造商提出了 17 个关于其如何监视用户的问题</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question. The questions covered issues such as recording or transcribing audio, taking screenshots, storing sensitive information, and sharing data with the cloud. The effort followed accusations involving LG and was intended to obtain clearer answers than lengthy privacy policies provide.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。问题涉及录音或保存音频转录、截取屏幕、在电视上存储敏感信息，以及与云端共享数据等事项。调查起因之一是针对 LG 的监视指控，目的是获得比冗长隐私政策更清晰的回答。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50453474' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>237</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50451903" data-id="50451903" data-tags="Hardware|Security" data-score="336" data-comments="131" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903" data-time="1794394120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.kate.land/images/ga100-block-diagram.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.kate.land' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">84</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using voltage glitching to unleash the A100 in my mining card</span><span class='zh'>通过电压故障注入解锁挖矿卡中的 A100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.kate.land</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX. The author used it to turn an 8GB CMP 170HX into a card functionally similar to an A100-80G, enabling roughly 120–126 functional SMs instead of the 108 used in production A100s. The method can also unlock features including NVLink, PCIe Gen4, NVJPG, NVDEC, and MIG, although several of those features were not specifically tested.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。作者利用这一点，将一张 8GB 的 NVIDIA CMP 170HX 改造成在功能上类似 A100-80G 的显卡，并启用了约 120 至 126 个可用 SM，而量产 A100 使用的是 108 个。该方法还可以解锁 NVLink、PCIe Gen4、NVJPG、NVDEC 和 MIG 等功能，但其中几项尚未经过专门测试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>336</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>131</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50486748" data-id="50486748" data-tags="AI" data-score="334" data-comments="92" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486748" data-time="1794591010">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6eeccf216579fe2181ae737d30f31abd7590125c/210_58_1759_1407/master/1759.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=334efc63d7472a97d72be0ffd23af8bb' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theguardian.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">85</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI unlocks Gödel&#x27;s notebooks, said to contain hundreds of proved theorems</span><span class='zh'>AI 解读哥德尔笔记本，据称其中包含数百个已证明定理</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kurt Gödel left 1,009 pages of densely written material, much of it in an obsolete shorthand, and the notebooks are said to contain hundreds of proved theorems. Gödel’s published incompleteness theorems transformed mathematics and helped pave the way for the digital age. His unpublished work spans tens of thousands of pages and remains of interest to historians of science.</span><span class='zh'>库尔特·哥德尔留下了 1,009 页密集书写的材料，其中许多使用已过时的速记，据称笔记本里包含数百个已证明定理。哥德尔发表的不完备性定理改变了数学，并为数字时代铺平了道路。他未发表的作品多达数万页，至今仍受到科学史学者关注。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>334</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486748' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>92</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50387796" data-id="50387796" data-tags="Education|Security" data-score="332" data-comments="54" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50387796" data-time="1793935634">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pwn.college/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pwn.college/themes/dojo_theme/static/img/dojo/ninja.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pwn.college' data-c='#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">86</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pwn.college/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pwn.college</span><span class='zh'>Pwn.college</span></a><span class="dom">pwn.college</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pwn.college is a free, hands-on education platform for learning core cybersecurity skills.</span><span class='zh'>Pwn.college 是一个免费的实践型平台，用于学习网络安全核心技能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pwn.college is a free, hands-on education platform for learning core cybersecurity skills. Learners practice through terminal- and systems-oriented challenges while progressing through a belt-based curriculum. The platform is maintained by hackers at Arizona State University and supports the university&#x27;s cybersecurity teaching as well as participation from learners worldwide.</span><span class='zh'>Pwn.college 是一个免费的实践型平台，用于学习网络安全核心技能。学习者通过面向终端和计算机系统的挑战进行练习，并沿着分级腰带课程逐步进阶。该平台由亚利桑那州立大学的黑客团队维护，既服务于该校网络安全教学，也向全球学习者开放。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>332</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50387796' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>54</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429024" data-id="50429024" data-tags="AI" data-score="330" data-comments="140" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429024" data-time="1794247126">
<a class="thumb" href="https://research.meta.ai/blog/muse-ember" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://research.meta.ai/articles/muse-glint/hero/blog-header-light-v1.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='research.meta.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.meta.ai/blog/muse-ember" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muse Ember: Our smallest, most efficient model</span><span class='zh'>Muse Ember：我们最小、最高效的模型</span></a><span class="dom">research.meta.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta introduced Muse Ember as its smallest and most efficient model for complex reasoning execution and repetitive agent work.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 发布了 Muse Ember，称其为用于复杂推理执行和重复性智能体工作的最小、最高效模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta introduced Muse Ember as its smallest and most efficient model for complex reasoning execution and repetitive agent work. The target workloads include tool calls, coding subtasks, routing, extraction, and long-document parsing. Meta says it matches Claude Haiku 5.5&#x27;s best score at about one-third the cost and beats every listed GPT-6 Luna setting.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 发布了 Muse Ember，称其为用于复杂推理执行和重复性智能体工作的最小、最高效模型。目标工作负载包括工具调用、编程子任务、路由、信息抽取和长文档解析。Meta 称，该模型以约三分之一的成本达到 Claude Haiku 5.5 的最佳成绩，并超过所列出的 GPT-6 Luna 各项设置。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>330</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429024' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>140</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50446246" data-id="50446246" data-tags="Education|Science" data-score="328" data-comments="58" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50446246" data-time="1794344324">
<a class="thumb" href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/six-math-essentials.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=teorth.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>teorth.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">88</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/six-math-essentials.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Six Math Essentials</span><span class='zh'>Terence Tao 的六个数学要点</span></a><span class="dom">teorth.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier. The book covers numbers, algebra, geometry, probability, analysis, and dynamics as gateways to quantitative thought, abstraction, calculation, uncertainty, scale, and change. No errata have been recorded for the book.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。全书分别以数字、代数、几何、概率、分析和动力学为入口，讨论定量思维、抽象、计算、不确定性、尺度与变化。目前该书尚无勘误记录。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Writing is presented as a tool for thinking.</span><span class='zh'>文章将写作视为一种思考工具，重点讨论如何通过写作发展并厘清想法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Writing is presented as a tool for thinking. The piece focuses on using writing to develop and clarify ideas.</span><span class='zh'>文章将写作视为一种思考工具，重点讨论如何通过写作发展并厘清想法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hugovk.dev/blog/2026/lock-github-actions/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lock Your GitHub Actions</span><span class='zh'>锁定你的 GitHub Actions</span></a><span class="dom">hugovk.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles. Previously, users were advised to pin actions to a full Git commit SHA because tags can be changed and redirected to malicious code in supply-chain attacks. Lockfiles provide another mechanism for constraining the actions used by a workflow.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。此前，建议用户将 action 固定到完整的 Git 提交 SHA，因为标签可以被修改，攻击者可能借此在供应链攻击中将原本安全的标签指向恶意代码。锁定文件为限制工作流所使用的 actions 提供了另一种机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.moddb.com/mods/openq4/downloads/openq4-0141-windows-x64" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Abandoned Quake 4 expansion finished by modders and now playable</span><span class='zh'>被放弃的《Quake 4》扩展包由模组作者完成并已可游玩</span></a><span class="dom">moddb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The never-released Quake 4 expansion is now playable through openQ4 0.14.1, the Awakening Edition.</span><span class='zh'>从未正式发行的《Quake 4》扩展包如今可通过 openQ4 0.14.1“觉醒版”游玩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The never-released Quake 4 expansion is now playable through openQ4 0.14.1, the Awakening Edition. The free, open-source replacement engine includes a second campaign, stable shadow mapping, a rebuilt interface, Vulkan support on Windows, smarter bots, and an experimental Android app. It also supports the original campaign and multiplayer on current Windows, Linux, and Steam Deck systems.</span><span class='zh'>从未正式发行的《Quake 4》扩展包如今可通过 openQ4 0.14.1“觉醒版”游玩。这款免费开源的替代引擎加入了第二战役、稳定的阴影映射、重制界面、Windows Vulkan 支持、更智能的机器人以及实验性的 Android 应用。它还支持在当前的 Windows、Linux 和 Steam Deck 系统上运行原版战役和多人模式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neil Brown describes running decoded.legal, a small English law firm focused on internet, telecommunications, and technology law, with Free software on both the client and server sides.</span><span class='zh'>Neil Brown 介绍了如何在客户端和服务器端都使用自由软件，运营 decoded.legal 这家专注于互联网、电信和科技法律的小型英格兰律师事务所。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neil Brown describes running decoded.legal, a small English law firm focused on internet, telecommunications, and technology law, with Free software on both the client and server sides. He began doing so around five years ago and has used Free software personally for much longer. The account presents the firm’s software approach as an ongoing practice.</span><span class='zh'>Neil Brown 介绍了如何在客户端和服务器端都使用自由软件，运营 decoded.legal 这家专注于互联网、电信和科技法律的小型英格兰律师事务所。他大约五年前开始这样做，而个人使用自由软件的时间更久。这篇文章记录了该事务所持续采用这一软件方式的实践。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://krthr.co/person-of-interest/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Person of Interest</span><span class='zh'>疑犯</span></a><span class="dom">krthr.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author reflects on watching the television series Person of Interest and connects it with memories of the early 2010s internet, including the SOPA and PIPA controversy and the Megaupload shutdown.</span><span class='zh'>作者回顾了观看电视剧《疑犯追踪》的经历，并联想到 2010 年代初的互联网，包括 SOPA 和 PIPA 争议以及 Megaupload 被关闭事件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author reflects on watching the television series Person of Interest and connects it with memories of the early 2010s internet, including the SOPA and PIPA controversy and the Megaupload shutdown. The show also prompts comparisons between its fictional Machine and technologies that now appear closer to reality.</span><span class='zh'>作者回顾了观看电视剧《疑犯追踪》的经历，并联想到 2010 年代初的互联网，包括 SOPA 和 PIPA 争议以及 Megaupload 被关闭事件。这部剧也让作者将剧中的虚构“机器”与如今看起来更接近现实的技术联系起来。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.freightwaves.com/news/11-4-million-in-nvidia-hardware-vanishes-during-shipment-from-california-to-texas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$11.4M in Nvidia hardware vanishes during shipment from CA to TX</span><span class='zh'>价值 1,140 万美元的英伟达硬件在从加州运往德州途中失踪</span></a><span class="dom">freightwaves.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas. The truck was collected on August 29, made several extended stops, and was last tracked in San Bernardino County. Oakland police confirmed an ongoing grand-theft investigation, while the carrier’s identity and the circumstances remain unclear.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。卡车于 8 月 29 日提货，途中多次长时间停留，最后在圣贝纳迪诺县失去踪迹。奥克兰警方已确认正在调查这起重大盗窃案，但承运方身份和货物失踪经过仍不清楚。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ableton presents Live 13 with experimental sound-design tools, precision mixing effects, and workflow improvements.</span><span class='zh'>Ableton 介绍了 Live 13，其中包括实验性声音设计工具、精确混音效果和工作流改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ableton presents Live 13 with experimental sound-design tools, precision mixing effects, and workflow improvements. The new step-sequencer clips support beats, melodies, and polyrhythms. Live 13 also adds support for ARA 2 plug-ins for faster and more advanced audio editing.</span><span class='zh'>Ableton 介绍了 Live 13，其中包括实验性声音设计工具、精确混音效果和工作流改进。新的步进音序器片段支持节拍、旋律和复节奏。Live 13 还支持 ARA 2 插件，以实现更快速、更高级的音频编辑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings. It runs in the background without joining calls, and keeps transcripts, recordings, and summaries on the user’s computer. The app also offers calendar integration and a local MCP server for optionally sharing notes with external frontier models.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。它在后台运行，不会加入通话，会议转录、录音和摘要也不会离开用户的电脑。该应用还提供日历集成和本地 MCP 服务器，用户可以选择通过它将笔记分享给外部前沿模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>285</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50406292" data-id="50406292" data-tags="Show HN|Security|Programming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="64" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406292" data-time="1794072450">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hedgerow-dev/coldframe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Coldframe, a honeypot that poses as a supermarket fridge controller</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Coldframe，伪装成超市冰柜控制器的蜜罐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces. It uses only the Python standard library and matches details such as headers, page sizes, certificate fields, and TLS versions found on real devices. The honeypot recognizes several exploit attempts, including the CVE-2025-41450 login bypass, and records submitted codes.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。它仅使用 Python 标准库，并模拟真实设备的请求头、页面大小、证书字段和 TLS 版本等细节。该蜜罐能够识别多种攻击尝试，包括 CVE-2025-41450 登录绕过，并记录提交的代码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406292' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>64</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50433216" data-id="50433216" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="231" data-comments="77" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50433216" data-time="1794263441">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Process Explorer for macOS</span><span class='zh'>适用于 macOS 的 Process Explorer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience. It provides a live process tree, module, handle, and thread inspection, per-process properties, system-wide performance graphs, and code-signing checks. The Swift, SwiftUI, and AppKit application uses public macOS APIs, with optional support for a privileged helper.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。它提供实时进程树、模块、句柄和线程检查、逐进程属性、系统级性能图表以及代码签名检查。该应用使用 Swift、SwiftUI 和 AppKit 构建，并通过 macOS 公共 API 工作，还可选择使用特权辅助程序。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>231</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50433216' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>77</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50391145" data-id="50391145" data-tags="Open Source|Code" data-score="164" data-comments="112" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50391145" data-time="1793965206">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Unleash/unleash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;ve built an open-source feature flag server for 11 years</span><span class='zh'>我们打造开源功能开关服务器已有 11 年</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released. It supports smaller, more manageable production deployments and can streamline software delivery. The project has been developed for 11 years.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。它支持更小、更易管理的生产部署，并可简化软件交付流程。该项目已开发 11 年。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>164</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50391145' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>112</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50427085" data-id="50427085" data-tags="Business|Hardware|Code" data-score="164" data-comments="84" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50427085" data-time="1794238741">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/datareceipts/data-receipts/tree/main/109-gpu-pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/datareceipts/data-receipts/tree/main/109-gpu-pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The same H100 costs $2.69 or $12.29 an hour depending where you rent it</span><span class='zh'>同一块 H100 的租用价格可能是每小时 2.69 或 12.29 美元</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour. For identical GPUs, it reports a 4.57× gap between the cheapest and most expensive options. Running one GPU continuously for a month costs $1,964 or $8,972 depending only on the provider.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。对于相同 GPU，最便宜和最昂贵选项之间的价格差达到 4.57 倍。连续运行一块 GPU 一个月，费用可能因服务商不同而为 1964 美元或 8972 美元。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>164</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50427085' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>84</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460154" data-id="50460154" data-tags="Hardware|Programming|Code" data-score="140" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154" data-time="1794438158">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tylertms/Stela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A general purpose Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs</span><span class='zh'>面向 x86-64 PC 的通用 Apple Silicon 层</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs. It includes an Arm64-to-x86-64 translator, an NVIDIA Metal driver, and a model of Apple silicon hardware, and can currently boot macOS and most apps. The v0.1.0 release is slow, requires more testing, and has a low chance of booting on current hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。它包含 Arm64 到 x86-64 的转换器、NVIDIA Metal 驱动以及 Apple 芯片硬件模型，目前可以启动 macOS 和大多数应用。v0.1.0 运行速度较慢，仍需大量测试，在当前硬件上成功启动的概率也很低。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>140</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50449891" data-id="50449891" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI|Code" data-score="138" data-comments="60" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891" data-time="1794373007">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adrbn/bo2-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Black Ops II statically recompiled to native ARM64 macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator. The game reportedly runs at nearly 60 frames per second across the campaign, local multiplayer with bots, and local Zombies mode, including owned DLC maps. Split-screen is being worked on, while LAN mode is planned for later.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。据称，战役、本地机器人多人模式和本地僵尸模式都能以接近 60 帧运行，已拥有的 DLC 地图也可使用。开发者正在处理分屏功能，局域网模式则计划稍后加入。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>138</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>60</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50471650" data-id="50471650" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Data|Code" data-score="134" data-comments="37" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471650" data-time="1794507348">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>134</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50471650' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>37</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50458166" data-id="50458166" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="132" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166" data-time="1794428637">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/MACaQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Huawei releases MACaQ, its internal quantizer for local inference</span><span class='zh'>华为发布用于本地推理的内部量化器 MACaQ</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models. It supports llama.cpp, vLLM, Diffusers, and PyTorch. Huawei says it can quantize a 122-billion-parameter model in 64 minutes using 32.5 GB of device memory, while achieving lower perplexity than MR-GPTQ and DuQuant++ in stated comparisons.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。它兼容 llama.cpp、vLLM、Diffusers 和 PyTorch。华为称，MACaQ 可使用 32.5 GB 设备内存，在 64 分钟内完成一个 1220 亿参数模型的量化；在其给出的比较中，困惑度低于 MR-GPTQ 和 DuQuant++。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>132</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50410249" data-id="50410249" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="131" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410249" data-time="1794109639">
