<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dmunozv04/isponsorblocktv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ISponsorBlockTV: SponsorBlock client for all YouTube TV clients</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV：适用于所有 YouTube TV 客户端的 SponsorBlock 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API. It can also mute videos automatically and press YouTube&#x27;s &quot;Skip Ad&quot; button when it appears. YouTube appears to have changed its screen-ID format and is revoking existing codes, so devices must be paired again.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。它还可以自动静音，并在 YouTube 广告出现“跳过广告”按钮后自动点击。YouTube 似乎更改了屏幕 ID 格式并正在撤销现有代码，因此设备需要重新配对。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410249' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50484007" data-id="50484007" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="131" data-comments="39" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50484007" data-time="1794580120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3 – A standalone XMPP server written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3：用 Rust 编写的独立 XMPP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features. It remains a pre-1.0, single-node server, while full XMPP compliance and clustering are still on the roadmap. The release expands secure federation without yet providing those larger deployment features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。它仍是一个处于 1.0 之前阶段的单节点服务器，完整 XMPP 兼容性和集群支持仍在路线图中。此次更新增强了安全联邦能力，但尚未提供这些更大规模部署功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50484007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>39</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50450007" data-id="50450007" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="114" data-comments="76" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007" data-time="1794374754">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">238</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kurtzer&#x27;s Ginux is now a userspace kernel</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kurtzer 的 Ginux 现已成为用户态内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances. It runs as an ordinary Linux process and defines an explicit, versioned contract between managed workloads and the host kernel. Its earlier phase as an independent Rust kernel booting through UEFI is complete, with that source preserved separately while active development continues on the userspace version.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。它作为普通 Linux 进程运行，并在受管理工作负载与主机内核之间建立明确且有版本控制的契约。此前通过 UEFI 启动、以 Rust 编写的独立内核阶段已经结束，相关源代码被单独保留，当前开发转向用户态版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>114</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>76</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50426207" data-id="50426207" data-tags="Programming|Web|Code" data-score="109" data-comments="40" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207" data-time="1794234840">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ikey4u/zebkit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">244</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ikey4u/zebkit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zebkit – Chromium as an embeddable library</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit——可嵌入的 Chromium 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows. It includes Blink, V8 with its JIT tiers, PDFium, FFmpeg, and other Chrome components, but omits the Chrome browser and Electron runtime. Applications can use it through C, C++, Rust, Python, Go, or JavaScript bindings.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。它包含 Blink、带有各级 JIT 的 V8、PDFium、FFmpeg 及其他 Chrome 组件，但不包含 Chrome 浏览器本身或 Electron 运行时。应用可以通过 C、C++、Rust、Python、Go 或 JavaScript 绑定使用它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>109</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50468944" data-id="50468944" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|AI|Web|Code" data-score="107" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468944" data-time="1794497152">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chocoford/ExcalidrawZ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ: Local Native Excalidraw on macOS/iOS</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ：macOS/iOS 原生本地版 Excalidraw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas. It adds native document management, iCloud sync, history, export, AI assistance, MCP integration, and other Apple-platform features. Drawings are handled as application documents rather than loose browser downloads.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。它增加了原生文件管理、iCloud 同步、历史记录、导出、AI 辅助、MCP 集成及其他 Apple 平台功能。绘图会作为应用文档管理，而不是浏览器中零散下载的文件。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust. It generates assembly with a compiler installed locally, so it does not require an internet connection.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。它使用本地安装的编译器生成汇编代码，因此无需互联网连接。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jjba23/sift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sift: LSP, Lint, Format Lisp, Scheme and Emacs Lisp</span><span class='zh'>Sift：用于 Lisp、Scheme 和 Emacs Lisp 的 LSP、Lint 与格式化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp. It reports malformed Lisp source and rewrites whitespace while preserving comments and read-time forms in their original positions. Formatting an already formatted file produces no further changes, a property asserted across the test suite.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。它会报告格式错误的 Lisp 源码并重写空白，同时保留注释和读取时形式的原始位置。对已经格式化的文件再次格式化不会产生变化，该特性在整个测试套件中都有断言验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>82</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>14</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50379221" data-id="50379221" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="81" data-comments="40" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379221" data-time="1793887591">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/piercefreeman/waymark" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">291</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/piercefreeman/waymark" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Waymark – A workflow engine that compiles jobs instead of replaying</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Waymark——编译任务而非重放任务的工作流引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS. It lets developers write workflows in ordinary Python code using conditionals, loops, and asyncio-style parallelism, while the runtime handles distributed execution. Rather than replaying past state in an interpreter, Waymark walks the program’s AST and compiles the logic into bytecode for a stackless virtual machine.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。开发者可以用普通 Python 代码编写工作流，包括条件语句、循环和类似 asyncio 的并行操作，再由运行时负责分布式执行。Waymark 不通过解释器重放历史状态，而是遍历程序 AST，将逻辑编译为运行在无栈虚拟机上的字节码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>81</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379221' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50416640" data-id="50416640" data-tags="Programming|Web|Code" data-score="76" data-comments="17" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50416640" data-time="1794168606">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lukks/like-webrtc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Peer-to-peer WebRTC connections with built-in signaling and discovery</span><span class='zh'>支持内置信令与发现机制的点对点 WebRTC 连接</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project provides peer-to-peer WebRTC connections with built-in signaling and peer discovery.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目提供带有内置信令和节点发现功能的点对点 WebRTC 连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project provides peer-to-peer WebRTC connections with built-in signaling and peer discovery. It works across browsers, Node.js, and React Native.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目提供带有内置信令和节点发现功能的点对点 WebRTC 连接。它支持浏览器、Node.js 和 React Native。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shikokuchuo/pymizu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mizu – zero-copy parallel R and Python over shared memory</span><span class='zh'>Mizu：通过共享内存实现 R 与 Python 的零拷贝并行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory. Its benchmarks report reducing task round trips from roughly 100 microseconds over sockets to less than 1 microsecond. Lists, dictionaries, NumPy arrays, and Arrow tables can be shared between Python and R as zero-copy views.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。其基准测试称，任务往返时间可从基于套接字的约 100 微秒降至不到 1 微秒。列表、字典、NumPy 数组和 Arrow 表都可以作为零拷贝视图在 Python 与 R 之间共享。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/popvax/yash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cancer vaccine for Yash, he&#x27;s diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer</span><span class='zh'>Yash 的癌症疫苗：他被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry. Yashdeep Bindal was diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer in January 2025 at age 32, shortly after returning to work following the birth of his daughter Maya.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。Yashdeep Bindal 于 2025 年 1 月、32 岁时被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌；此前他刚在女儿 Maya 出生后重返工作岗位。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/klad-app/klad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1445832118/919683d0-c60e-41f7-9913-132f49b4bdd6' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">313</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/klad-app/klad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Klad, a paint app your coding agent can drive (source-available)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Klad，一款可由编码代理驱动的绘画应用（源代码可用）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans. It supports brush painting, frame-by-frame animation, bones and inverse kinematics, vectors, text layers, colors, layer effects, and persistent unlimited undo. Its editable strokes are intended to let agents create drafts that people can refine stroke by stroke.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。它支持笔刷绘画、逐帧动画、骨骼与反向运动学、矢量、文本图层、颜色、图层效果，以及可跨文件重新打开保留的无限撤销。它将可编辑笔触作为核心，使代理能够生成草稿，再由人类逐笔修改。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>69</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454951' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>29</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50405263" data-id="50405263" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="64" data-comments="12" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405263" data-time="1794064561">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Video-Rebirth/hyperflow-h3-flash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: We compressed a 31B video+audio diffusion model to 14B, 6 steps, INT8</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将 31B 视频音频扩散模型压缩至 14B、6 步、INT8</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters. It generates video and synchronized stereo audio together and supports INT8 and GGUF formats. The project also provides weights, ComfyUI nodes, a GGUF setup guide, and an eight-step LoRA for the full-size model.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。它能够同时生成视频和同步立体声，并支持 INT8 与 GGUF 格式。项目还提供模型权重、ComfyUI 节点、GGUF 配置指南，以及适用于完整模型的 8 步 LoRA。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>64</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>12</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50377613" data-id="50377613" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="58" data-comments="8" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50377613" data-time="1793875528">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyphatech/spindle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Spindle – a full-featured, type-safe web framework for OCaml 5</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Spindle，面向 OCaml 5 的全功能类型安全 Web 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas. It includes an RFC-strict HTTP server, WebSockets, SSE, MCP server primitives, tracing, metrics, and logging. Its benchmarks report performance two times faster than NestJS and five times faster than FastAPI on a single core, although speed was not the project’s primary goal.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。它包含严格遵循 RFC 的 HTTP 服务器、WebSocket、SSE、MCP 服务器基础组件，以及分布式追踪、指标和日志功能。基准测试显示，它在单核上运行速度约为 NestJS 的两倍、FastAPI 的五倍，不过速度并非项目的首要目标。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>58</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50377613' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>8</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50409855" data-id="50409855" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="53" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50409855" data-time="1794103347">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/crmne/zapfast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZapFast: A fast, native WhatsApp client in Rust and egui</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast：使用 Rust 和 egui 构建的快速原生 WhatsApp 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZapFast is a lightweight, native WhatsApp client written in Rust with an egui interface.</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast 是一款使用 Rust 编写、基于 egui 界面的轻量级原生 WhatsApp 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZapFast is a lightweight, native WhatsApp client written in Rust with an egui interface. It is designed to run on Linux, macOS, and Windows. The project provides downloads, setup instructions, a user guide, and contribution information.</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast 是一款使用 Rust 编写、基于 egui 界面的轻量级原生 WhatsApp 客户端。它面向 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 平台。项目提供下载、安装说明、用户指南和参与贡献的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/Micron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Micron – Oberon with the Power of C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Micron——带有 C 能力的 Oberon</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability. Its goal is to use one language for boot code, device drivers, operating-system kernels, and ordinary applications without a garbage collector. It supports explicit memory layouts, signed and unsigned integers, pointer operations, and direct C interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。它的目标是用同一种语言编写启动代码、设备驱动、操作系统内核和普通应用，同时不依赖垃圾回收器。该语言支持显式内存布局、有符号和无符号整数、指针操作以及直接的 C 互操作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>53</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413266' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50411083" data-id="50411083" data-tags="Science|Programming|Code" data-score="47" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411083" data-time="1794121742">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graealex/WaveDudeNext" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext 2D Wave Simulator</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 二维波模拟器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics. Users can turn images into refractive scenes, place light sources and optical components, and observe refraction, diffraction, and interference. It displays both the wave field and time-averaged intensity, while supporting probes, timeline control, video export, adjustable brightness, custom color schemes, and multiple interface languages.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。用户可以将图像转换为折射场景，放置光源和光学元件，并观察折射、衍射与干涉现象。程序同时显示波场和时间平均强度，并支持探针、时间轴控制、视频导出、亮度调节、自定义配色以及多种界面语言。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>47</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411083' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>11</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50443264" data-id="50443264" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Data|DevOps|Code" data-score="46" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264" data-time="1794330969">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTSLM/TimeNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TimeNet: Open-source data infrastructure for Temporal Foundation Models</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet：面向时间基础模型的开源数据基础设施</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models. It supports tasks including classification, question answering, scalar prediction, temporal localization, forecasting, generation, editing, and correspondence. The project reports that training on multiple temporal datasets improved average F1 performance by 14% for OpenTSLM and 22% for SLIP.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。它支持分类、问答、标量预测、时间定位、预测、生成、编辑和对应关系等任务。项目方称，在多个时间数据集上训练后，OpenTSLM 的平均 F1 性能提升了 14%，SLIP 提升了 22%。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>46</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50415078" data-id="50415078" data-tags="Security|Open Source|Education|Code" data-score="43" data-comments="12" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415078" data-time="1794157795">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/vsmutok/FuckCaptcha" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">367</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vsmutok/FuckCaptcha" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A field guide to CAPTCHA types and how they break</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 类型及其破解方式实地指南</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives. It provides a searchable web version and frames the material for education and authorized research. Its stated goal is to make changing CAPTCHA systems easier to navigate, compare, and cite while emphasizing responsible use.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。项目将材料定位为教育和获授权研究用途。其目标是在强调负责任使用的同时，让不断变化的 CAPTCHA 系统更易于查找、比较和引用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>43</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415078' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>12</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50429144" data-id="50429144" data-tags="Show HN|AI|DevOps|Code" data-score="40" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429144" data-time="1794247544">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Dodaios/ste80-humanizer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: STE80 Humanizer – de-AI text by writing 80% of the way to ASD-STE100</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer：将 AI 文本改写至接近 ASD-STE100 的 80%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning. It targets roughly 80% compliance with ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English. The example focuses on Kubernetes liveness probes and retains both their restart behavior and the warning that misconfiguration can cause cascading restarts.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。它的目标是让文本达到约 80% 的 ASD-STE100 简明技术英语规范。示例内容围绕 Kubernetes 存活探针展开，并保留了探针触发重启的行为以及配置错误可能导致级联重启的警告。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/IainKay/ram-altman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RAM-Altman reports unused RAM on your machine, one email a day</span><span class='zh'>RAM-Altman 每天发一封邮件，向 Altman 报告机器上的闲置内存</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RAM-Altman is a Bash script that once a day reports unused RAM directly to OpenAI CEO Sam Altman.</span><span class='zh'>RAM-Altman 是一个每天向 OpenAI CEO Sam Altman 报告闲置内存的 Bash 脚本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RAM-Altman is a Bash script that once a day reports unused RAM directly to OpenAI CEO Sam Altman. It requires no installation and provides sample emails through its companion site.</span><span class='zh'>RAM-Altman 是一个每天向 OpenAI CEO Sam Altman 报告闲置内存的 Bash 脚本。它无需安装，并通过配套网站提供示例邮件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/no382001/zipc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A ZIP-style Prolog VM for trilog</span><span class='zh'>用于 trilog 的 ZIP 风格 Prolog 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C. Its goal is to provide enough of a VM to compare it with the current trilog runtime. ZIP is the abstract machine described in the 1984 book Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。其目标是先搭建足够完整的虚拟机，以便与当前的 trilog 运行时进行比较。ZIP 是 1984 年《Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine》中描述的抽象机器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Christ10-8/reflota" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Reflota – Open-source data recovery for NTFS/FAT disks with phased copy</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Reflota——支持分阶段复制的 NTFS/FAT 开源数据恢复工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files. It reads disks directly: for NTFS, it reconstructs the folder tree from the master file table and directory metadata, while for FAT and exFAT it reads directories and follows cluster chains. It is intended for cases where Windows reports that a disk is unformatted, a folder will not open, or Explorer hangs.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。它会直接读取磁盘：处理 NTFS 时读取主文件表并根据目录元数据重建文件夹树，处理 FAT 和 exFAT 时读取目录并跟踪簇链。当 Windows 报告磁盘未格式化、文件夹无法打开或资源管理器卡死时，该工具可用于尝试恢复数据。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>30</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50482455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50486914" data-id="50486914" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="28" data-comments="7" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486914" data-time="1794591543">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/basetenlabs/deepseek-ultrafast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware. It supports a 1-million-token context length and multi-server KV-cache sharing. The project measures decode speed, step time, and acceptance length using a Tetris-generation prompt.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。它支持 100 万 token 的上下文长度和多服务器 KV 缓存共享。项目使用生成俄罗斯方块的提示词测量解码速度、步骤耗时和接受长度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>28</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486914' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>7</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50354763" data-id="50354763" data-tags="Data|Programming|Code" data-score="27" data-comments="3" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50354763" data-time="1793725892">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ndrean/ZeBridge" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZeBridge – PostgreSQL replicas on devices, synchronized over NATS</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge：通过 NATS 同步设备上的 PostgreSQL 副本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS. It keeps a local database on each device and queues writes while the device is offline. When connectivity returns, PostgreSQL remains authoritative by applying rules, resolving conflicts, and returning a result to the application.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。它在每台设备上保留本地数据库，并在离线时排队保存写入操作。重新连接后，PostgreSQL 仍是权威数据源，负责应用规则、解决冲突并向应用返回结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>27</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50354763' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50413970" data-id="50413970" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="22" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413970" data-time="1794150727">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sshah03/recalc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Recalc – Spreadsheet where cells can be Claude Code or Codex terminals</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Recalc——单元格可以运行 Claude Code 或 Codex 的电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session. Sessions can communicate through grid cells, named cells, or an MCP server, and can be continued in a regular terminal. The tool currently runs locally on macOS, with Linux support described as less tested.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。不同会话可以通过网格单元格、命名单元格或 MCP 服务器互相通信，也可以在普通终端中继续运行。目前该工具可在 macOS 本地运行，Linux 支持则尚未充分测试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>22</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413970' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>5</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50401844" data-id="50401844" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Hardware|Code" data-score="21" data-comments="7" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50401844" data-time="1794028591">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rkaehn/uniform" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: JavaScript web apps with WASM layout and GPU rendering in 280 KB</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 WASM 布局和 GPU 渲染构建 280 KB 的 JavaScript 网页应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB. Its creator aims to avoid the platform limits of native frameworks, the HTML/CSS constraints of browser-engine approaches, and the size or language requirements of other cross-platform frameworks. The design bundles the UI framework with the application so developers can update it independently of platform release cycles.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。作者希望避免原生框架的平台限制、基于浏览器引擎方案所继承的 HTML/CSS 约束，以及其他跨平台框架的体积或语言要求。该设计将 UI 框架直接与应用打包，使开发者能够独立于平台发布周期更新它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>21</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50401844' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>7</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50427153" data-id="50427153" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="16" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50427153" data-time="1794239099">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Neighborhood-Grid/flatpoints" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: 3D to 2D, point clouds flattening (geometry images)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：3D 转 2D，展平点云（几何图像）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds. It maps each 3D point from a point cloud sampled on an unknown surface to a 2D pixel, arranging the points into a square grid. The resulting flat representation can serve as an image-like indexing structure.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。它把从未知曲面采样得到的每个三维点映射为二维像素，并将这些点排列到方形网格中。生成的平面表示可以作为类似图像的索引结构。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project is a model zoo of blood-glucose forecasters trained and tested on the author’s own data and on the public Metabonet dataset.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project is a model zoo of blood-glucose forecasters trained and tested on the author’s own data and on the public Metabonet dataset. It includes 15 forecasting models, including models developed by the author. Most of the repository’s code was generated with AI.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。项目包含 15 个预测模型，其中包括作者自行开发的模型。代码仓库中的大部分代码由 AI 生成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SafeScript/safescript" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SafeScript, a safe version of JavaScript for executing untrusted code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：SafeScript——用于执行不可信代码的安全版 JavaScript</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments. It excludes features including eval, new, globals, and prototype access, then compiles to plain JavaScript and PHP. The compiler is about 48 KB minified, or roughly 12 KB gzipped, and uses .safe files intended to be locally verifiable.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。它排除了 eval、new、全局变量和原型访问等功能，并可编译为普通 JavaScript 和 PHP。编译器压缩后约 48 KB、gzip 后约 12 KB，使用 .safe 文件格式，并支持本地验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50417478' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50405688" data-id="50405688" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="10" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405688" data-time="1794067672">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support. The project also provides an associated planning board.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。项目还提供了配套的规划看板。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WolveKafka – Consume Kafka with a plain HTTP endpoint</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：WolveKafka——通过纯 HTTP 接口消费 Kafka</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language. It manages consumer groups, batching, retries, offsets, and dead-letter queues. The project includes a quickstart, examples, deployment guidance, and comparison material.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。它负责管理消费者组、批处理、重试、偏移量和死信队列。项目还提供快速入门、示例、部署指南和对比资料。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/agda/agda/releases/tag/v2.9.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agda v2.9.0</span><span class='zh'>Agda v2.9.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode. The release also includes substantial performance improvements and drops support for GHC 8.8, 8.10, and 9.0. Agda is a dependently typed programming language and interactive theorem prover.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。该版本还带来了显著的性能改进，并停止支持 GHC 8.8、8.10 和 9.0。Agda 是一种依赖类型编程语言和交互式定理证明器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ori-drs/drs-vpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DRS-VPT. Localize a photo in a 3D point cloud without an initial pose</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：DRS-VPT——无需初始位姿，在三维点云中定位照片</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics. The same model works across driving LiDAR scans, indoor iPhone photos, and structure-from-motion maps. The authors provide the code, model, weights, and an online demo.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。同一个模型可用于驾驶场景的 LiDAR 扫描、室内 iPhone 照片和运动恢复结构地图。作者提供了代码、模型、权重以及在线演示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/davide-troiani/moeshift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>M4 Pro vs. M5 Pro (24GB): Qwen 3.8 Flash Next at 374 Tok/S Prefill with MoEShift</span><span class='zh'>M4 Pro 对比 M5 Pro（24GB）：Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 使用 MoEShift 达到 374 Tok/s 预填充速度</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models. On a Mac mini with an M4 Pro and 24 GB of unified memory, it runs the 125-billion-parameter Qwen 3.8 Flash Next using under 10 GB of peak process memory. The project reports roughly half the peak memory usage of Strata and 2.6× faster long-prompt processing.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。在配备 M4 Pro 和 24GB 统一内存的 Mac mini 上，它运行 1250 亿参数的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 时，进程峰值内存低于 10GB。项目称，与 Strata 相比，它的峰值内存占用约少一半，长提示处理速度快 2.6 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50432586' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50474106" data-id="50474106" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="8" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106" data-time="1794516784">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Cerulion – ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime with multi-process replay</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Cerulion——支持 ROS 2、带多进程回放的机器人运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust. It aims to reduce inter-process communication latency without requiring a single process or restricting messages to plain-old-data types. Its multi-process graphs can be deterministically resimulated, and existing ROS 2 nodes and launch files can run alongside native Cerulion Rust or Python code.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。它旨在降低进程间通信延迟，同时不要求将程序合并为单进程，也不限制消息必须使用普通数据类型。其多进程图可以进行确定性重演，现有 ROS 2 节点和 launch 文件也能与原生 Cerulion Rust 或 Python 代码并行运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>8</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50492269" data-id="50492269" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="7" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50492269" data-time="1794617098">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Getterbetter/ember/releases/tag/v1.0.1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Light control for MacBook charger status light (open-source)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：开源控制 MacBook 充电器状态灯</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger. It supports turning the light on or off automatically based on time or Focus status, as well as keeping it persistently on or off. The project invites feature suggestions through GitHub.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。它支持根据时间或专注模式状态自动开关灯，也可以让灯持续亮起或关闭。项目通过 GitHub 征集功能建议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RenderBear/haci" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: My compact, bio inspired distributed edge detection algorithm</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的紧凑型仿生分布式边缘检测算法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex. Its current benchmark results fall between traditional methods such as Canny and Sobel and modern deep neural networks, while retaining the interpretability of the former. The author plans to improve video frame rates and performance across different hardware, and is seeking collaborators.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。目前的基准测试结果介于 Canny、Sobel 等传统方法与现代深度神经网络之间，同时保留了传统方法的可解释性。作者计划提升视频处理帧率和不同硬件上的性能，并寻找合作伙伴。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OmniJev/Flide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Flide: Make Jev-like decision models up to 9.5x faster</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Flide，让 Jev 类决策模型最高提速 9.5 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models. The project claims up to a 9.5x speedup over vLLM and SGLang and currently supports more than 30 popular local Jev-like models. It can be installed with pip using the package name flide.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。项目称其速度最高可达 vLLM 和 SGLang 的 9.5 倍，目前支持 30 多种热门本地 Jev 类模型。用户可以通过 pip 安装名为 flide 的软件包。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>5</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50421155' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50410253" data-id="50410253" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410253" data-time="1794109684">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmSach/maple" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Maple – Sub-2-BPW Moe Pruning to Run Gemma 4 26B at 45 TPS on a 6GB GPU</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Maple——次于 2 BPW 的 MoE 剪枝，让 Gemma 4 26B 在 6GB GPU 上达到 45 TPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models. It compresses Gemma 4 26B-A4B from 9.92 GB to 4.75 GB, equivalent to 1.48 effective bits per parameter. The project reports 100% in-VRAM execution on 6 GB laptop GPUs at 45.4 tokens per second.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。它将 Gemma 4 26B-A4B 从 9.92 GB 压缩到 4.75 GB，相当于每参数 1.48 个有效比特。项目报告称，该模型可在 6GB 笔记本 GPU 上完全载入显存运行，速度达到每秒 45.4 个 token。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50447169" data-id="50447169" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="3" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50447169" data-time="1794349523">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/awake-client/awake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awake-client/awake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Git friendly open API client</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections. The project began as a side project and became an everyday tool for its creators. It is presented as a Git-friendly open API client.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。该项目最初是一个业余项目，后来成为创建者日常使用的工具。它被定位为一款对 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端。</span></p>
